# Evidentiary Hearing Transcript, DPU 23-80 / DPU 23-81 (Unitil), Volume 3, February 5, 2024

**Source**: madpu_23-80_20240206_transcript_evidentiary_hearing_vol3_20240205.pdf
**Pages**: 66 PDF pages. PDF pp. 1-46 carry transcript pages 144-321 (cover = Tr. p. 144; PDF p. 2 = Tr. pp. 145-148; and so on, four transcript pages per PDF page; PDF p. 46 = Tr. p. 321). PDF pp. 47-66 are the reporter's word index.
**Date**: Monday, February 5, 2024, commencing at 10:03 a.m.
**Reporter**: Alan H. Brock, RDR, CRR, Farmer Arsenault Brock LLC, 617.728.4404

_Extractor note (not source text): This transcript is converted mechanically from the PDF text layer and every transcript page 145-321 was checked to be present once, with consecutive line numbers 1-24. Each transcript page is reproduced as a block that keeps the printed line numbers, so a quote can be cited as "Tr. page:line" (for example Tr. 204:5). Running headers and footers (reporter name, case caption, "Pages 145..148", the "YVer1f" tag) are removed. The transcript's own typography uses a mid-dot for spacing; it is replaced by plain spaces. Transcription spellings and the reporter's own typos (for example "2381" for "23-81" at Tr. 147:5) are kept as printed. The word index at the end contains 10,178 internal hyperlinks to transcript pages; they are not reproduced. The PDF has no images or charts._

 8     Q. Give me one moment, please.
 9             Thank you for the response,
10 Mr. Nawazelski.
11             MS. ARCOVIO: Mr. Hearing Officer, can
12 we have just a moment?
13             MR. TASSONE: Sure. Let's go off the
14 record for a moment.
15             (Discussion off the record.)
16             MR. TASSONE: Let's go back on the
17 record.
18     Q. Mr. Amen, earlier this morning Mr. Taylor
19 stated that the impact of the calendarization of the
20 billing determinant was 0.15 percent on the electric
21 side. Can you please provide a comparable number
22 for the gas side? And I could take that as a record
23 request, if you don't have the answer at hand.
24     A. [AMEN] Okay.
